Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any extragalactic module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 986. The projective resolution has length at most 909 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the extragalactic space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 986 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 909. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the extragalactic structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 763. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 575 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).
